<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Saeed (Radi Allahu Anhu) aur Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne ek shakhs ko Khaibar par aamil muqarrar kiya, wo umdah khajoor lekar aaya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne poocha: Kya sab khajoorein Khaibar ki aisi hi hoti hain? Usne kaha: Nahi Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), hum is khajoor mein se ek saa do saa ke badle mein ya do saa teen saa ke badle mein khareed liya karte hain. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Aisa na karo, pehle buri khajoor ko rupayon ke badle mein bech kar phir umdah khajoor rupay dekar khareed lo.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1311]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1311" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 419

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1312)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، أَنَّ زَيْدًا أَبَا عَيَّاشٍ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ، سَأَلَ سَعْدَ بْنَ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ عَنِ الْبَيْضَاءِ، بِالسُّلْتِ فَقَالَ لَهُ سَعْدٌ أَيَّتُهُمَا أَفْضَلُ قَالَ الْبَيْضَاءُ ‏.‏ فَنَهَاهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ سَعْدٌ سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُسْأَلُ عَنِ اشْتِرَاءِ التَّمْرِ بِالرُّطَبِ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ أَيَنْقُصُ الرُّطَبُ إِذَا يَبِسَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا نَعَمْ ‏.‏ فَنَهَى عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Yazid that Zayd ibn Ayyash told him that he had once asked Sad ibn Abi Waqqas about selling white wheat for a type of good barley. Sad asked him which was the better and when he told him the white wheat, he forbade the transaction. Sad said, &quot;I heard the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, being asked about selling dried dates for fresh dates, and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Do the dates diminish in size when they become dry?&#x27; When he was told that they did, he forbade that</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1312 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन अबू अय्यश से रिवायत है के उन्होंने साद बिन आबि वक़्क़ास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से पुछा के जो हिजाज़ में पैदा होता है उसके बदले में बेच सकते हैं? उन्होंने कहा: दोनों में कौनसा अच्छा है? वो बोले: जो. तोह मन किया इस से और साद ने कहा मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सुना पुछा के खुश्क खजूर को रातब (ताज़ा) के बदले में बेचना कैसा है? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: रातब जब सूख जाता है तोह वज़न कम हो जाता है? लोगों ने कहा: हाँ. तोह आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मन फ़रमाया.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن ابوعیاش سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے پوچھا سعد بن ابی وقاص سے کہ جو کے غور اور حجاز میں پیدا ہوتا ہے کے بدلے میں بیچ سکتے ہیں انہوں نے کہا دونوں میں کونسا اچھا ہے بولے جو تو منع کیا اس سے اور سعد نے کہا میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے سنا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے لوگوں سے پوچھا کہ خشک کھجور کو رطب بدلے میں بیچنا کیسا ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا رطب جب سوکھ جاتا ہے تو وزن اس کا کم ہو جاتا ہے لوگوں نے کہا ہاں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع فرمایا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Abu Ayyash se riwayat hai ke unhone Saad bin Abi Waqqas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se poocha ke jo Hijaz mein paida hota hai uske badle mein bech sakte hain? Unhone kaha: Dono mein kaunsa achha hai? Wo bole: Jo. Toh mana kiya is se aur Saad ne kaha maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se suna, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne logon se poocha ke khushk khajoor ko ratab (taaza) ke badle mein bechna kaisa hai? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ratab jab sookh jata hai toh wazan kam ho jata hai? Logon ne kaha: Haan. Toh aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne mana farmaya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1312]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1312" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 420

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1313)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنِ الْمُزَابَنَةِ وَالْمُزَابَنَةُ بَيْعُ الثَّمَرِ بِالتَّمْرِ كَيْلاً وَبَيْعُ الْكَرْمِ بِالزَّبِيبِ كَيْلاً ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1313]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1313" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1313</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1313 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade muzabana. Muzabana was selling fresh dates for dried dates by measure, and selling grapes for raisins by measure</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1313]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1313" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मज़बाना से मन फ़रमाया. मज़बाना उसको कहते हैं के दरख़्त पर फल (खजूर या अंगूर) का अंदाज़ा करके खुश्क खजूर या अंगूर के बदले में फरोख्त की जाये.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1313]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1313" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کیا مزابنہ اس کو کہتے ہیں کہ درخت پر پھل کھجور یا انگور اندزہ کر کے خشک کھجور یا انگور کے بدلے میں فروخت کی جائیں ۔</p>

## Narration 421

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1314)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ دَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ، عَنْ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ، مَوْلَى ابْنِ أَبِي أَحْمَدَ عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنِ الْمُزَابَنَةِ وَالْمُحَاقَلَةِ وَالْمُزَابَنَةُ اشْتِرَاءُ الثَّمَرِ بِالتَّمْرِ فِي رُءُوسِ النَّخْلِ وَالْمُحَاقَلَةُ كِرَاءُ الأَرْضِ بِالْحِنْطَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1314]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1314" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1314</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1314 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Da&#x27;ud ibn al-Husayn from Abu Sufyan, the mawla of Ibn Abi Ahmad, from Abu Said al-Khudri that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade muzabana and muhaqala. Muzabana was selling fresh dates for dried dates while they were still on the trees. Muhaqala was renting land in exchange for wheat</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1314]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1314" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सईद खुदरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मज़बाना और मुहकला से मना फ़रमाया. मज़बाना के मानि ऊपर बयान हुए और मुहकला उसको कहते हैं कि गेहूं का खेत बदले में खुश्क गेहूं के बचे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1314]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1314" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو سعید خدری سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کیا مزابنہ اور محاقلہ سے مزابنہ کے معنی اوپر بیان ہوئے اور محاقلہ اس کو کہتے ہیں کہ گہیوں کا کھیت بدلے میں خشک گہیوں کے بیچے ۔</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنِ الْمُزَابَنَةِ وَالْمُحَاقَلَةِ وَالْمُزَابَنَةُ اشْتِرَاءُ الثَّمَرِ بِالتَّمْرِ وَالْمُحَاقَلَةُ اشْتِرَاءُ الزَّرْعِ بِالْحِنْطَةِ وَاسْتِكْرَاءُ الأَرْضِ بِالْحِنْطَةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ فَسَأَلْتُ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ عَنِ اسْتِكْرَاءِ الأَرْضِ بِالذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ فَقَالَ لاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ نَهَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنِ الْمُزَابَنَةِ وَتَفْسِيرُ الْمُزَابَنَةِ أَنَّ كُلَّ شَىْءٍ مِنَ الْجِزَافِ الَّذِي لاَ يُعْلَمُ كَيْلُهُ وَلاَ وَزْنُهُ وَلاَ عَدَدُهُ ابْتِيعَ بِشَىْءٍ مُسَمًّى مِنَ الْكَيْلِ أَوِ الْوَزْنِ أَوِ الْعَدَدِ وَذَلِكَ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ يَكُونُ لَهُ الطَّعَامُ الْمُصَبَّرُ الَّذِي لاَ يُعْلَمُ كَيْلُهُ مِنَ الْحِنْطَةِ أَوِ التَّمْرِ أَوْ مَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ مِنَ الأَطْعِمَةِ أَوْ يَكُونُ لِلرَّجُلِ السِّلْعَةُ مِنَ الْحِنْطَةِ أَوِ النَّوَى أَوِ الْقَضْبِ أَوِ الْعُصْفُرِ أَوِ الْكُرْسُفِ أَوِ الْكَتَّانِ أَوِ الْقَزِّ أَوْ مَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ مِنَ السِّلَعِ لاَ يُعْلَمُ كَيْلُ شَىْءٍ مِنْ ذَلِكَ وَلاَ وَزْنُهُ وَلاَ عَدَدُهُ فَيَقُولُ الرَّجُلُ لِرَبِّ تِلْكَ السِّلْعَةِ كِلْ سِلْعَتَكَ هَذِهِ أَوْ مُرْ مَنْ يَكِيلُهَا أَوْ زِنْ مِنْ ذَلِكَ مَا يُوزَنُ أَوْ عُدَّ مِنْ ذَلِكَ مَا كَانَ يُعَدُّ فَمَا نَقَصَ عَنْ كَيْلِ كَذَا وَكَذَا صَاعًا - لِتَسْمِيَةٍ يُسَمِّيهَا - أَوْ وَزْنِ كَذَا وَكَذَا رِطْلاً أَوْ عَدَدِ كَذَا وَكَذَا فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَعَلَىَّ غُرْمُهُ لَكَ حَتَّى أُوفِيَكَ تِلْكَ التَّسْمِيَةَ فَمَا زَادَ عَلَى تِلْكَ التَّسْمِيَةِ فَهُوَ لِي أَضْمَنُ مَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ عَلَى أَنْ يَكُونَ لِي مَا زَادَ ‏.‏ فَلَيْسَ ذَلِكَ بَيْعًا وَلَكِنَّهُ الْمُخَاطَرَةُ وَالْغَرَرُ وَالْقِمَارُ يَدْخُلُ هَذَا لأَنَّهُ لَمْ يَشْتَرِ مِنْهُ شَيْئًا بِشَىْءٍ أَخْرَجَهُ وَلَكِنَّهُ ضَمِنَ لَهُ مَا سُمِّيَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ الْكَيْلِ أَوِ الْوَزْنِ أَوِ الْعَدَدِ عَلَى أَنْ يَكُونَ لَهُ مَا زَادَ عَلَى ذَلِكَ فَإِنْ نَقَصَتْ تِلْكَ السِّلْعَةُ عَنْ تِلْكَ التَّسْمِيَةِ أَخَذَ مِنْ مَالِ صَاحِبِهِ مَا نَقَصَ بِغَيْرِ ثَمَنٍ وَلاَ هِبَةٍ طَيِّبَةٍ بِهَا نَفْسُهُ فَهَذَا يُشْبِهُ الْقِمَارَ وَمَا كَانَ مِثْلَ هَذَا مِنَ الأَشْيَاءِ فَذَلِكَ يَدْخُلُهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمِنْ ذَلِكَ أَيْضًا أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ لَهُ الثَّوْبُ أَضْمَنُ لَكَ مِنْ ثَوْبِكَ هَذَا كَذَا وَكَذَا ظِهَارَةَ قَلَنْسُوَةٍ قَدْرُ كُلِّ ظِهَارَةٍ كَذَا وَكَذَا - لِشَىْءٍ يُسَمِّيهِ - فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَعَلَىَّ غُرْمُهُ حَتَّى أُوفِيَكَ وَمَا زَادَ فَلِي ‏.‏ أَوْ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ أَضْمَنُ لَكَ مِنْ ثِيَابِكَ هَذِي كَذَا وَكَذَا قَمِيصًا ذَرْعُ كُلِّ قَمِيصٍ كَذَا وَكَذَا فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَعَلَىَّ غُرْمُهُ وَمَا زَادَ عَلَى ذَلِكَ فَلِي ‏.‏ أَوْ أَنْ يَقُولُ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ لَهُ الْجُلُودُ مِنْ جُلُودِ الْبَقَرِ أَوِ الإِبِلِ أُقَطِّعُ جُلُودَكَ هَذِهِ نِعَالاً عَلَى إِمَامٍ يُرِيهِ إِيَّاهُ ‏.‏ فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ مِائَةِ زَوْجٍ فَعَلَىَّ غُرْمُهُ وَمَا زَادَ فَهُوَ لِي بِمَا ضَمِنْتُ لَكَ ‏.‏ وَمِمَّا يُشْبِهُ ذَلِكَ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ عِنْدَهُ حَبُّ الْبَانِ اعْصُرْ حَبَّكَ هَذَا فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ كَذَا وَكَذَا رِطْلاً فَعَلَىَّ أَنْ أُعْطِيَكَهُ وَمَا زَادَ فَهُوَ لِي ‏.‏ فَهَذَا كُلُّهُ وَمَا أَشْبَهَهُ مِنَ الأَشْيَاءِ أَوْ ضَارَعَهُ مِنَ الْمُزَابَنَةِ الَّتِي لاَ تَصْلُحُ وَلاَ تَجُوزُ ‏.‏ وَكَذَلِكَ - أَيْضًا - إِذَا قَالَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ لَهُ الْخَبَطُ أَوِ النَّوَى أَوِ الْكُرْسُفُ أَوِ الْكَتَّانُ أَوِ الْقَضْبُ أَوِ الْعُصْفُرُ أَبْتَاعُ مِنْكَ هَذَا الْخَبَطَ بِكَذَا وَكَذَا صَاعًا مِنْ خَبَطٍ يُخْبَطُ مِثْلَ خَبَطِهِ أَوْ هَذَا النَّوَى بِكَذَا وَكَذَا صَاعًا مِنْ نَوًى مِثْلِهِ وَفِي الْعُصْفُرِ وَالْكُرْسُفِ وَالْكَتَّانِ وَالْقَضْبِ مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ فَهَذَا كُلُّهُ يَرْجِعُ إِلَى مَا وَصَفْنَا مِنَ الْمُزَابَنَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1315" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade muzabana and muhaqala. Muzabana was selling fresh dates for dried dates. Muhaqala was buying unharvested wheat in exchange for threshed wheat and renting land in exchange for wheat. Ibn Shihab added that he had asked Said ibn al-Musayyab about renting land for gold and silver. He said, &quot;There is no harm in it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade muzabana. The explanation of muzabana is that it is buying something whose number, weight and measure is not known with something whose number, weight or measure is known, for instance, if a man has a stack of food whose measure is not known, either of wheat, dates, or whatever food, or the man has goods of wheat, date kernels, herbs, safflower, cotton, flax, silk, and does not know its measure or weight or number and then a buyer approaches him and proposes that he weigh or measure or count the goods, but, before he does, he specifies a certain weight, or measure, or number and guarantees to pay the price for that amount, agreeing that whatever falls short of that amount is a loss against him and whatever is in excess of that amount is a gain for him. That is not a sale. It is taking risks and it is an uncertain transaction. It falls into the category of gambling because he is not buying something from him for something definite which he pays. Everything which resembles this is also forbidden.&quot; Malik said that another example of that was, for instance, a man proposing to another man, &quot;You have cloth. I will guarantee you from this cloth of yours so many hooded cloaks, the measureof each cloak to be such-and-such, (naming a measurement). Whatever loss there is, is against me and I will fulfill you the specified amount and whatever excess there is, is mine.&quot; Or perhaps the man proposed, &quot;I will guarantee you from this cloth of yours so many shirts, the measurement of each shirt to be such-and-such, and whatever loss there is, is against me and I will fulfill the specified amount and whatever excess there is, is mine.&quot; Or perhaps a man proposed to a man who had cattle or camel hides, &quot;I will cut up these hides of yours into sandals on a pattern I will show you. Whatever falls short of a hundred pairs, I will make up its loss and whatever is over is mine because I guaranteed you.&quot; Another example was that a man say to a man who had ben-nuts, &quot;I will press these nuts of yours. Whatever falls short of such-and-such a weight by the pound, I will make it up, and whatever is more than that is mine.&quot; Malik said that all this and whatever else was like it or resembled it was in the category of muzabana, which was neither good nor permitted. It was also the same case for a man to say to a man, who had fodder leaves, date kernels, cotton, flax, herbs or safflower, &quot;I will buy these leaves from you in exchange for such-and-such a sa, (indicating leaves which are pounded like his leaves) . . or these date kernels for such-and-such a sa of kernels like them, and the like of that in the case of safflower, cotton, flax and herbs.&quot; Malik said, &quot;All this is what we have described of muzabana</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1315" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझे मालिक से, उन्होंने इब्न शिहाब से, उन्होंने सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब से बयान किया के रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने मुज़ाबना और मुहाक़ला से मना फ़रमाया। मुज़ाबना ताज़ा खजूरों को ख़ुश्क खजूरों के बदले बेचना है। मुहाक़ला बग़ैर कटी हुई गंदुम को कटी हुई गंदुम के बदले खरीदना और ज़मीन को गंदुम के बदले किराये पर देना है। इब्न शिहाब ने मज़ीद कहा के उन्होंने सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब से सोने और चांदी के बदले ज़मीन किराये पर लेने के बारे में पूछा तो उन्होंने कहा: &#x27;इस में कोई हर्ज नहीं।&#x27; मालिक ने कहा: &#x27;रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने मुज़ाबना से मना फ़रमाया। मुज़ाबना की वज़ाहत यह है के ऐसी चीज़ का सौदा करना जिस का वज़न, पैमाना या तादाद मालूम न हो, उस चीज़ के बदले जिस का वज़न या पैमाना मालूम हो, मसलन किसी के पास अनाज का ढेर हो जिस का वज़न मालूम न हो और वो उसे एक ख़ास मिक़दार कह कर बेच दे।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1315" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے، انہوں نے ابن شہاب سے، انہوں نے سعید بن المسیب سے بیان کیا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مزابنہ اور محاقلہ سے منع فرمایا۔ مزابنہ تازہ کھجوروں کو خشک کھجوروں کے بدلے بیچنا ہے۔ محاقلہ بغیر کٹی ہوئی گندم کو کٹی ہوئی گندم کے بدلے خریدنا اور زمین کو گندم کے بدلے کرائے پر دینا ہے۔ ابن شہاب نے مزید کہا کہ انہوں نے سعید بن المسیب سے سونے اور چاندی کے بدلے زمین کرائے پر لینے کے بارے میں پوچھا تو انہوں نے کہا: &#x27;اس میں کوئی حرج نہیں۔&#x27; مالک نے کہا: &#x27;رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مزابنہ سے منع فرمایا۔ مزابنہ کی وضاحت یہ ہے کہ ایسی چیز کا سودا کرنا جس کا وزن، پیمانہ یا تعداد معلوم نہ ہو، اس چیز کے بدلے جس کا وزن یا پیمانہ معلوم ہو، مثلاً کسی کے پاس اناج کا ڈھیر ہو جس کا وزن معلوم نہ ہو اور وہ اسے ایک خاص مقدار کہہ کر بیچ دے۔&#x27;</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1315" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhe Malik se, unhone Ibn Shihab se, unhone Saeed ibn al-Musayyab se bayan kiya ke Rasool Allah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam ne muzabana aur muhaqala se mana farmaya. Muzabana taza khajuron ko khushk khajuron ke badle bechna hai. Muhaqala baghair kati hui gandum ko kati hui gandum ke badle kharidna aur zameen ko gandum ke badle kiraye par dena hai. Ibn Shihab ne mazeed kaha ke unhone Saeed ibn al-Musayyab se sone aur chandi ke badle zameen kiraye par lene ke bare mein pucha to unhone kaha: &#x27;Is mein koi harj nahi.&#x27; Malik ne kaha: &#x27;Rasool Allah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam ne muzabana se mana farmaya. Muzabana ki wazahat yeh hai ke aisi cheez ka sauda karna jis ka wazan, paimana ya tadaad maloom na ho, us cheez ke badle jis ka wazan ya paimana maloom ho, maslan kisi ke paas anaj ka dher ho jis ka wazan maloom na ho aur woh use ek khaas miqdar keh kar bech de.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1315" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

## Narration 423

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1317)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ أَبِي تَمِيمٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الْحُبَابِ، سَعِيدِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الدِّينَارُ بِالدِّينَارِ وَالدِّرْهَمُ بِالدِّرْهَمِ لاَ فَضْلَ بَيْنَهُمَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1317]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1317" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1317</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1317 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Musa ibn Abi Tamim from Abu&#x27;l Hubab Said ibn Yasar from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;A dinar for a dinar, a dirham for a dirham, no excess between the two</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1317]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1317" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: एक दीनार को एक दीनार के बदले में बेचो और एक दिरहैम को एक दिरहम के बदले में</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1317]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1317" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ایک دینار کو ایک دینار کے بدلے میں بیچو اور ایک درہم کو ایک درہم کے بدلے میں نہ زیادہ کے بدلے میں ۔</p>

## Narration 424

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1318)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تَبِيعُوا الذَّهَبَ بِالذَّهَبِ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَلاَ تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ وَلاَ تَبِيعُوا الْوَرِقَ بِالْوَرِقِ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَلاَ تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ وَلاَ تَبِيعُوا مِنْهَا شَيْئًا غَائِبًا بِنَاجِزٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1318" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1318</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1318 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abu Said al-Khudri that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do not sell gold for gold except like for like and do not increase one part over another part. Do not sell silver for silver, except like for like and do not increase one part over another part. Do not sell some of it which is not there for some of it which is</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1318" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1318 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सईद खुदरी (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: सोने के बदले में सोना मत बेचो मगर बराबर, ना ज्यादा करो एक दूसरे पर और चांदी के बदले में चांदी मत बेचो मगर बराबर, ना ज्यादा करो एक दूसरे पर और इस में से कुछ नकद वादा पर मत बेचो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1318" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو سعید خدری سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا مت بیچو سونے کے بدلے میں سونا مگر برابر نہ زیادہ کرو ایک دوسرے پر اور مت بیچو چاندی کے بدلے میں چاندی کے مگر برابر نہ زیادہ کرو ایک دوسرے پر نہ بیچو کچھ اس میں سے نقد وعدہ پر ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Saeed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Sone ke badle mein sona mat becho magar barabar, na zyada karo ek dusre par aur chandi ke badle mein chandi mat becho magar barabar, na zyada karo ek dusre par aur is mein se kuch naqad waada par mat becho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

## Narration 425

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1320)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنْ جَدِّهِ، مَالِكِ بْنِ أَبِي عَامِرٍ أَنَّ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ، قَالَ قَالَ لِي رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تَبِيعُوا الدِّينَارَ بِالدِّينَارَيْنِ وَلاَ الدِّرْهَمَ بِالدِّرْهَمَيْنِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1320" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard from his grandfather, Malik ibn Abi Amir that Uthman ibn Affan said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to me, &#x27;Do not sell a dinar for two dinars nor a dirham for two dirhams</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1320" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझे मालिक से बयां किया कि उन्होंने अपने दादा मालिक इब्न आबि आमिर से सुना के उथमण इब्न अफ्फान रदिअल्लहु अन्हु ने कहा: &#x27;रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने मुझ से फ़रमाया: एक दीनार के बदले दो दीनार न बेचो और न ही एक दिरहम के बदले दो दिरहैम.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1320" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے بیان کیا کہ انہوں نے اپنے دادا مالک بن ابی عامر سے سنا کہ عثمان بن عفان رضی اللہ عنہ نے کہا: &#x27;رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مجھ سے فرمایا: ایک دینار کے بدلے دو دینار نہ بیچو اور نہ ہی ایک درہم کے بدلے دو درہم۔&#x27;</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1320" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhe Malik se bayan kiya ke unhone apne dada Malik ibn Abi Amir se suna ke Uthman ibn Affan Radiallahu Anhu ne kaha: &#x27;Rasool Allah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam ne mujh se farmaya: Ek dinar ke badle do dinar na becho aur na hi ek dirham ke badle do dirham.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1320" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
